**Permanent consumers:**

- [LeanInfoTheory/Examples/ChannelCode.lean](../../LeanInfoTheory/Examples/ChannelCode.lean): [`unequal_input_masses_differ`](../../LeanInfoTheory/Examples/ChannelCode.lean#L130), [`unequal_supported_collision`](../../LeanInfoTheory/Examples/ChannelCode.lean#L137).
- [LeanInfoTheory/Examples/ChannelCoding.lean](../../LeanInfoTheory/Examples/ChannelCoding.lean): [`repeated_input_correlated`](../../LeanInfoTheory/Examples/ChannelCoding.lean#L79), [`repeated_error`](../../LeanInfoTheory/Examples/ChannelCoding.lean#L100), [`repeated_fano_to_capacity`](../../LeanInfoTheory/Examples/ChannelCoding.lean#L153).
- [LeanInfoTheory/Examples/ChannelCodingAsymptotic.lean](../../LeanInfoTheory/Examples/ChannelCodingAsymptotic.lean): [`alternating_complete_interface`](../../LeanInfoTheory/Examples/ChannelCodingAsymptotic.lean#L443), [`four_finite_nonvacuous`](../../LeanInfoTheory/Examples/ChannelCodingAsymptotic.lean#L295), [`four_public_eventual`](../../LeanInfoTheory/Examples/ChannelCodingAsymptotic.lean#L307), [`intermittent_unbounded_spikes`](../../LeanInfoTheory/Examples/ChannelCodingAsymptotic.lean#L377), [`intermittent_no_eventual_error_floor`](../../LeanInfoTheory/Examples/ChannelCodingAsymptotic.lean#L398), [`intermittent_public_frequent_obstruction`](../../LeanInfoTheory/Examples/ChannelCodingAsymptotic.lean#L417).

**Producer/source connection:** C17 memorylessChannel supplies conditional output rows at actual encoder words, without making q iid. Q carries original uniform messages and the encoder graph; its input/output marginal is channelJoint(U.map encoder,K). Actual-law Markov and decoder processing yield both DPIs. Fano uses uniform first marginal of J; C22 arbitrary-q repeated capacity bounds the very same encoder pushforward via C18 arbitrary-input information bounds and capacity. C20 codeRate converts the undivided nats inequality only for n>0. For one arbitrary family, finite Fano plus vanishing error derives2(C+log2) eventual rate bound before tolerance and guarded real limsup. Positive eventual lower-rate premise gives quantitative/floor obstructions; frequent fixed gap gives only nonvanishing, by contradiction with the tolerance cap.

Source anchors: [`memorylessChannel`](../../LeanInfoTheory/Probability/ProductChannel.lean#L122), [`channelExtension`](../../LeanInfoTheory/Probability/FiniteChannel.lean#L72), [`messageInputOutputLaw_map_inputOutput`](../../LeanInfoTheory/Shannon/ChannelCode.lean#L112), [`isMarkovChain_channelExtension`](../../LeanInfoTheory/Shannon/SemanticBridge/Markov.lean#L285), [`mutualInfo_messageOutputLaw_le_mul_channelCapacity`](../../LeanInfoTheory/Shannon/ChannelCoding.lean#L84), [`mutualInfo_fano_lower_bound_of_uniform_source`](../../LeanInfoTheory/Shannon/Fano.lean#L824), [`mutualInfo_channelJoint_memorylessChannel_le`](../../LeanInfoTheory/Shannon/ProductChannelCapacity.lean#L97), [`product_objective_le_sum_capacity`](../../LeanInfoTheory/Shannon/ProductChannelCapacity.lean#L37), [`mutualInfo_channelJoint_productChannel_le`](../../LeanInfoTheory/Shannon/SemanticBridge/ProductChannel.lean#L125), [`codeRate`](../../LeanInfoTheory/Shannon/CodingRate.lean#L32), [`eventually_codeRate_le_bound`](../../LeanInfoTheory/Shannon/ChannelCodingAsymptotic.lean#L50), [`limsup_codeRate_le_channelCapacity`](../../LeanInfoTheory/Shannon/ChannelCodingAsymptotic.lean#L103), [`not_tendsto_errorProbability_zero_of_frequently_channelCapacity_add_le_codeRate`](../../LeanInfoTheory/Shannon/ChannelCodingAsymptotic.lean#L177).

**Reference adaptation:**

- CT91 1991 section8.5 printed192-194/PDF214-216; PW24 August16,2024 section17.1 Definition17.1/(17.1) printed342-343/PDF367-368. Indexed total deterministic encoder/decoder, original uniform Fin M messages and explicit positive natural budgets. Use nats, retain colliding message identity and average error. No reserved erasure index, maximal-error equivalence or optimal decoder claim. Raw law finiteness is outputs only.
- Local C17 raw product/memoryless channel and existing PMF channel algebra. Conditional output coordinates factor at a supplied word; this never asserts independence of the encoder pushforward. Right-associated message/input/output law and cross-product Markov proof include null fibers without choosing conditional-law meanings.
- C18 arbitrary-input product-channel MI inequality; C22 ProductChannelCapacity; PW24 section6.1 Theorem6.1(a), printed106-107/PDF131-132. Product capacity upper bound ranges over every actual q, using its actual coordinate marginals. Product maximizers establish the capacity identity, not a necessary iid/injective premise on the code-induced q. C23 passes U.map encoder unchanged.
- CT91 section8.9 printed204-207/PDF226-229; PW24 section17.4 Theorem17.3 printed348-349/PDF373-374. Use existing undivided uniform-source Fano, with log2 nats replacing the textbook bit constant1. No division by1-Pe, so error1 is valid; only normalized/pointwise forms require n>0/R>0. The registered PW24 alternative-proof indicator/probability wording mismatch is not imported; canonical decoding-error semantics and actual message marginal remain authoritative.
- Existing C20 Shannon.CodingRate; CT91/PW24 code-rate conventions. codeRate n M=Real.log M/n with natural M. Its n=0 value is totalized zero, not normalization. No informal noninteger2^(nR) message counts or extra bit-valued duplicate APIs.
- CT91 section8.9 fixed-rate weak-converse reasoning; PW24 sections17.4 and19.1/start19.2 printed348-349,371-376/PDF373-374,396-401. Arbitrary dependent positive-budget family rather than a fixed-rate sequence. Explicitly derive eventual boundedness, then control error times a fixed bound before tolerance/real-limsup results. Operational-capacity definitions, expurgation and channel-achievability statements are excluded context. Extra finite-prefix mutation is optional under approved criteria; theorem generality still allows arbitrary finite-prefix behavior.
- mathlib v4.33.1 revision0df444a360eaa60ab8c11dca51a86af692955474. Filter.limsup_le_iff' for real rates requires IsCoboundedUnder and IsBoundedUnder; both are explicit, with atTop Nat nontrivial. Use eventual upper bound and global rate nonnegativity. Frequent arbitrary unbounded obstruction does not use limsup.
- CT918.9/PW2417.4 finite weak-converse motivation; project derivations. C<R plus eventual R<=rate gives an eventual positive error floor. A fixed positive gap only frequently gives failure of error->0, not an eventual floor or error->1. The frequent theorem is a local derivation by contradiction from the tolerance cap, not attributed verbatim to a numbered textbook result.

**Current disposition:** **SATISFIED** as an implementer source/consumer assessment supported by actual recorded parent checks. Independent status, reason and evidence are established only by the actual C24.03 completion and linked original report when present.

## Limits and remaining owners

No mathematical declaration, dependency, reviewed attribute, stable ID, baseline or earlier type artifact is changed. No independent verdict is supplied by implementation support. The 25 future process rows, cumulative clean-candidate qualification, rendered API milestone, expanded retained baseline, external consumer and publication remain under their authorized steps.
